All rows bind `LN-JP-V01 / ACQUIRED-20261017-A1`. `O###` resolves to stable `LN-JP-V01-O###` in the [frozen reading and exact locator index](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V01_DEEP_READING.md#observation-locator-index-and-shared-field-contract); the full source/locator hashes, scene chronology and first-encounter freezes live there. `R001` below means `RULE-LN-JP-001`, and similarly for the other registered rules. `EPI-*` denotes disclosure-ledger events. No shared category is inferred from an individual's private narration.

## Responsibility and boundaries

This ledger owns `A → B` state independently of `B → A`, plus shared-rule records only where shared acceptance is evidenced. A rule that one person believes exists may differ from a proposed, agreed, performed or enforced rule. The disclosure ledger owns who knows or believes it; the claims ledger owns interpretation of its significance; source readings own detailed interactions. A relationship label never substitutes for this directional evidence.

For each relevant observed interaction record initiative, response, refusal or accommodation, pressure and alternatives actually available, concealment or disclosure, action and consequence, and repair. Distinguish payment, practical obligations, requests, permissions, desires and consent to the particular act. Neither payment nor acquiescence alone proves desire or a general permission. Record ambiguity and contradictory behavior without resolving it through a preselected moral or diagnostic label.

## Append-only relational event schema

Identifiers: `REL-<witness>-<unit>-NNN`; rule identities `RULE-<witness>-NNN` remain stable across event revisions. Link to source-local observation IDs. Event time and analyst discovery time are separate; preserve each transition and prior state reference.

## Rule, boundary and commitment register schema

| `RULE-LN-JP-001` / v1→v2→v3 | Miyagi pays / Sendai visits | One visit ¥5,000; initial proposed frequency around once/twice weekly, not fixed duration: O002/O014; part0009 p5–12; part0011 p230–247 | Both accept usual transaction; wearer/owner negotiate one garment substitution O033 | v1 cash; v2 April shirt in lieu; v3 ordinary cash resumes O040, today's initially refused fee accepted O061 | REL001–003/007–008/014–015 | ACTIVE conventional frame; does not itself establish total obedience, desire or contact permission | All versions retained in events; no universal noncash convertibility or price change |
| `RULE-LN-JP-002` / v1 | Both / timing | After school, no days-off meetings; phone contact accepted: O014, part0011 p247–255 | Both assent; winter-break complaint/restatement O015; Miyagi distinguishes messages from meetings O031 | No blanket holiday-message ban; next-day visits accepted; Miyagi later limits own summons for exams, not a new unlimited-availability duty | REL001/008/010 | ACTIVE with practical scheduling accommodation | Weekly self-expectation is not mutually agreed minimum; actual future change unknown |
| `RULE-LN-JP-003` / v1 | Both / school approach | Initial 'will not approach at school' and assent: part0011 p253–255; mutual force expressly acknowledged part0021 p133–136 | Miyagi protests/renews; Sendai admits literal-subject sophistry | School partition; separate class changes routine but not its existence | REL001/012/014 | ACTIVE/asserted, KNOWINGLY BREACHED by Sendai; no accepted abolition shown | April first unsolicited home contact broke a habit, not this rule; school exception does not erase agreement |
| `RULE-LN-JP-004` / v1 | Both / secrecy and discoverability | Sendai invokes initial no-telling agreement: O007 part0009 p146–157; handwriting discovery refusal O021 part0012 p353–358 | Mutual reputational leverage; actual refusal of discoverable cooperation | Connection later visible at school, but payment/body arrangements not shown revealed | REL002/004/014 | Secret content retained as far as source establishes; outsider suspicion not omniscience | Do not treat every observed school interaction as complete disclosure |
